# EN: Faces for the 49 provincial governors of gg_province_governors.txt (22/9/2026), same method as
#     gg_dna.txt: the DNA of a lookalike (Americas Character DNA, Workshop 3008998036, first; vanilla
#     otherwise), with hair and eye colour set, style genes swapped from donors and the face shape moved
#     towards the period portrait (sources and licences: SOURCES_retratos.md, «Gobernadores»).
#     Hair colour is his colour in the years he governs, not in a later portrait. Style genes sit on
#     the middle of their accessory slot. Faces with no portrait follow the period's partisan fashion
#     (Roy Hora, «El bigote en Argentina, de Rosas a Yrigoyen», Anuario IEHS 36(1), 2021): rosistas the
#     federal moustache with long sideburns, unitarians and the Coalition shaven lips with sideburns or
#     the U beard, the rest sideburns; they say NOT PORTRAIT-BASED in their comment.
# ES: Caras de los 49 gobernadores provinciales, con el metodo de gg_dna.txt. El color de pelo es el de
#     los anios en que gobiernan. Los que no tienen retrato siguen la moda partidaria de la epoca (Hora,
#     2021): rosistas con bigote federal y patillas largas; unitarios y Coalicion con el labio afeitado y
#     patillas o barba en U; el resto con patillas. Dicen SIN RETRATO en su comentario.
